| `CL-B0245-001` | Sui initiates an experiment in self-presentation: the grammatical center is the girl she herself wants to become, rather than a demand from her peers that she abandon her current manner `THEMATIC_INTERPRETATION`. | Sui initiates an experiment in self-presentation: the grammatical center is the girl she herself wants to become, rather than a demand from her peers that she abandon her current manner.
